Algorithm:
def project_simplex_box(y, T):
   x = y.copy()
   lo = np.where(x < 0, x, 0)
   hi = np.where(x > 1, x, 1)
   low_v = lo.min()
   high_v = hi.max()
   for 40 iterations:
      mid=(low_v+high_v)/2
      z = np.clip(y+mid,0,1)
      s = z.sum()
      if s < T: low_v=mid else high_v=mid
   return np.clip(y+(low_v+high_v)/2,0,1)
This works for any y because as ν→-∞, z→0 sum0; ν→∞ sum n. Need initial interval: low_v = min(y)-1 - max(0,T?)? Actually to make sum <T: choose low_v = min(y) - 1 - T? But 40 iterations over [-10,10] maybe enough if y within few. After L-BFGS with penalty, y within [0,1] maybe. We can set low_v=np.min(y)-5, high_v=np.max(y)+5. But if y huge? Use while: low = min(y)-1.0; high=max(y)+1.0; if sum(clip(y+low)) > T: low -= sumdiff? Could. def c5_vec_fft(h,n):
  M=1
  while M < 4*n: M <<=1
  a=np.zeros(M); a[:n]=h
  b=np.zeros(M); b[:n]=1-h
  # corr = ifft(fft(a)*conj(fft(b)))
  A=np.fft.rfft(a); B=np.fft.rfft(b)? Complex? We need full. Use rfft for real saves.
```

  if k>=0:
    nv=n-k

  else: ...

Dense gradient with weights p length L:
```
def dense_grad_weighted(h,n,p):
  L=2*n-1
  M=1; while M<4*n: M<<=1
  w=np.zeros(M); w[:L]=p  # order d=-n+1..n-1
  wr=np.zeros(M); wr[:L]=p[::-1]
  x=np.zeros(M); x[:n]=h
  o=np.zeros(M); o[:n]=1.0
  X=np.fft.rfft(x); O=np.fft.rfft(o); W=np.fft.rfft(w); WR=np.fft.rfft(wr)
  T=np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(X), M)
  U=np.fft.irfft(WR*np.conj(X), M)
  S=np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(O), M)
  idx=np.arange(-(n-1), n) % M
  T=T[idx]; U=U[idx]; S=S[idx]
  grad=(S-T-U)*(2/n)
  return grad
```
Need verify T,U mapping: We tested T_i for i=0..n-1 corresponds idx τ=i-(n-1). `np.arange(-(n-1), n)` length 2n-1 has values -n+1, ..., n-1; %M gives indices. For i=0, τ=-n+1, indeed idx first. T[0] should T_0. Good.
But T array from corr(w,h) formula ∑ w[p]h[p+τ]. At τ=-n+1, target i=0. Good. U similar. S same.

Need normalize p. For dense gradient with p sum 1. If p has only one active, dense overkill; use sparse.

Objective active logsumexp:
```
def active_smooth_obj(h,n,lags,alpha,mu):
  c=c5_vec_fft(h,n)
  vals=c[lags]
  m=vals.max()
  s=alpha*(vals-m)
  # stable
  logsum = m + (np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s)))/alpha) ? Wait m + logsumexp(s)/alpha where s<=0, logsumexp(s)=logsumsum.
  # Actually objective = m + (log(sum exp(s)))/alpha.
  val = m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s))) / alpha
  if mu:
    diff=h.sum()-n/2
    val += mu*diff*diff
  return val
```
If s all very negative? s=0 for max, exp sum ≥1, fine. For alpha large, logsum≈log(count ties)/alpha.

- add penalty 2 mu diff.

Select active:
```
def select_lags(c, n, top_q=16, max_lag=32, tol_rel=1e-5, tol_abs=1e-8):
  m=c.max()
  tol=max(tol_abs, tol_rel*m)
  cand=np.where(c >= m - tol)[0]
  if len(cand)>max_lag:
     order=np.argsort(-c[cand])[:max_lag]
     cand=cand[order]
  else:
     if len(cand)<top_q:
        # add top
        top=np.argsort(-c)[:top_q]
        cand=np.unique(np.concatenate([cand,top]))
        if len(cand)>max_lag: cand=c[np.argsort(-c)??]
  return cand
```

def eval_pair_move(t, Ldiff, Q, c0):
    return np.max(c0 + t*Ldiff + t*t*Q)

Implement:
```
def make_Ldiff(h,n,i,j):
  L=np.zeros(2*n-1)
  # i first: slice [n-1-i : 2*n-1-i] += 1-h
  L[n-1-i : 2*n-1-i] += 1.0 - h
  # i second: slice [i : i+n] += -h[::-1]
  L[i : i+n] += -h[::-1]
  L[n-1-j : 2*n-1-j] -= 1.0 - h  # because subtract L_j? Wait L_i - L_j: for j contributions negative
  L[j : j+n] -= -h[::-1] => L[j:j+n] += h[::-1]
```

But note `h[::-1]` view, adding to slice with broadcast okay.

Periodic bitset local search may be faster and can find without solver. Let's design quick function `periodic_bit_search(p, K, max_iter=200000)` for multiple p. Use objective max D/E. Use bit_count. It can run in background within time. For p up to 64, eval 63 shifts bit ops. 100k iterations maybe ~1s? Let's estimate: 63 bit operations (shift, and, bit_count) per eval, 100k ->6.3M bit_count; maybe 0.5s. Good. We can do 1M ~5s. Use random restarts/hill climb.
```
def periodic_eval_mask(x,p,masks):
  full=(1<<p)-1
  best=0
  for r in 1..p-1:
    valid=(1<<(p-r))-1
    sh=(x<<r)&full
    D=(x & ~sh & valid).bit_count()
    if D>best: best=D
    E=((full^x) & sh & valid).bit_count()
    if E>best: best=E
    if best>K: can early exit? return best
  return best
```
For local search, maintain mask weight m. Random swap: choose random ones list and zeros list. Re-eval full (can early exit if >current?). We can do hill climb: if new max<current, accept; if stuck, accept with probability or do random restart. Since objective integer, target K small. Use temperature schedule. Need no closures. Need verify FFT numerics. Let's derive M: while M < 4*n: M <<=1. For n=500 M=2048. Good. Use `np.fft.rfft` on length M arrays. Potential memory. 
```
def compute_c5_fft(h,n):
    M=1
    while M < 4*n: M <<=1
    a=np.zeros(M); a[:n]=h
    b=np.zeros(M); b[:n]=1.0-h
    A=np.fft.rfft(a); B=np.fft.rfft(b)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(A*np.conj(B), n=M)
    L=2*n-1
    out=np.empty(L)
    out[0:n-1]=corr[M-(n-1):M]
    out[n-1]=corr[0]
    out[n:]=corr[1:n]
    out *= 2.0/n
    out[ (out < 0) & (out > -1e-12)] = 0.0
    return out
```
We could prealloc cache of M? Not.

- `project_feasible(h,n,T=None)` proper projection:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    y=np.array(h,dtype=float,copy=True)
    # initial low/high
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    # ensure sum at lo <=target and hi >=target
    # if not, expand
    for _ in range(10):
       s_lo=np.clip(y+lo,0,1).sum()
       if s_lo <= target: break
       lo -= target - s_lo + 1
    for _ in range(10):
       s_hi=np.clip(y+hi,0,1).sum()
       if s_hi >= target: break
       hi += target - s_hi +1
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.clip(y+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    x=np.clip(y+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    # final diff correction with room
    T=target
    for _ in range(20):
       diff=T-x.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-x; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          x += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          tot=x.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          x += (diff/tot)*x
    return np.clip(x,0,1)
```
Could be heavy for many calls but okay. For pair search, h already feasible; after moves exact. Use simpler `make_feasible` after L-BFGS.

- `build_L(h,n)`:
```
def build_L(h,n):
    Lmat=np.empty((n, 2*n-1), dtype=float)
    hrev = h[::-1]
    one_minus_h = 1.0 - h
    for i in range(n):
        row = Lmat[i]
        row[:] = 0.0
        row[n-1-i : 2*n-1-i] = one_minus_h
        row[i : i+n] += -hrev
    return Lmat
```
Need row initialization? `Lmat` uninitialized; set row zeros then slices. Could use `Lmat[i].fill(0.0)`. This is Python loop n. For n=1000, okay. But `-hrev` view; adding to slice. At d=0, first slice and second overlap one element in row? For i? The slices disjoint except maybe? They are different ranges that overlap? For row, first slice [n-1-i,2n-1-i], second [i,i+n]. They overlap for central indices. Using `+=` after first sets both contributions. Good.
Potential issue: `row[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] = one_minus_h` creates slice length n? start n-1-i, end 2n-1-i exclusive, length i? 2n-1-i - (n-1-i)=n. Good. `hrev` length n. For i=n-1 first [0,n] okay; i=0 [n-1,2n-1]. second [0,n]; [n-1,2n-1]. Good.

- `select_lags(c,n,tol_rel=1e-5,tol_abs=1e-9,max_active=64,top_q=16)`. Use c = cvec.
```
def select_lags(...):
  m=c.max()
  tol=max(tol_abs, tol_rel*m)
  cand=np.where(c >= m - tol)[0]
  if cand.size ==0: cand=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
  if cand.size > max_active:
      # order by c desc
      order=np.argsort(-c[cand])[:max_active]
      cand=cand[order]
  else:
      if cand.size < top_q:
          top=np.argsort(-c)[:top_q]
          cand=np.unique(np.concatenate((cand,top)))
      if cand.size > max_active:
          order=np.argsort(-c[cand])[:max_active]; cand=cand[order]
  return cand
```

- `sparse_grad_weighted(h,n,lags,weights)`: as above. Actually for active_smooth_grad, need weights p sum 1. Use loops.
```
def active_smooth_obj(h,n,lags,alpha,mu):
  c=compute_c5_fft(h,n)
  vals=c[lags]
  m=vals.max()
  s=alpha*(vals-m)
  # if alpha very high and s underflow? exp(s) okay s<=0
  logsum=np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s)))
  val=m + logsum/alpha
  if mu:
     d=h.sum()-n/2.0; val += mu*d*d
  return float(val)
```
`active_smooth_grad`:
```
  c=...
  vals=c[lags]
  m=vals.max(); s=alpha*(vals-m)
  w=np.exp(s); w /= w.sum()
  g=sparse_grad_weighted(h,n,lags,w)
  if mu: g += 2*mu*(h.sum()-n/2)*np.ones(n)
  return g
```

- `run_active_lbfgs(h,n,deadline,configs=None)` as above. We need avoid too long per minimize. Use options `maxiter` maybe 500, `maxfun` 1000. For each alpha config. Check time before each.
```
def run_active_lbfgs(h,n,deadline, configs=None):
  h=project_feasible(h,n); best=compute_c5_direct(h,n)
  if configs is None: configs=[(50,0.1,16),(200,1.0,16),(1000,10.0,32),(5000,100.0,32),(20000,1000.0,32)]
  bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
  for alpha,mu,maxa in configs:
    if time.time() > deadline-3: break
    c=compute_c5_fft(h,n)
    lags=select_lags(c,n,max_active=maxa)
    try:
      res=minimize(active_smooth_obj,h,jac=active_smooth_grad,args=(n,lags,alpha,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':800,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':1500,'maxls':50})
    except Exception: pass
    hnew=project_feasible(res.x,n)
    cnew=compute_c5_direct(hnew,n)
    if cnew < best: best=cnew; h=hnew
  return h,best
```
For n=1000, bounds list length 1000 okay. Use L-BFGS with function may call many times; each active grad loops over lags 32 * n slices. 1500 eval *32*1000=48M C ops, okay. But Python loop 32*1500=48k, okay.

def pair_local_search(h,n,deadline, top_g=36, stall_s=20):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c5_fft(h,n)
    best=float(c.max())
    last_imp=time.time()
    fails=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(12345) # for random pairs? deterministic
    while time.time() < deadline:
        # Build L
        L=build_L(h,n)
        active=select_lags(c,n,max_active=64)
        # grad = average L[:,active]
        grad = L[:, active].mean(axis=1) * (2.0/n)
        # top indices
        idxs = np.argsort(-np.abs(grad))[:top_g]
        # ensure enough unique; if less, add random
        if idxs.size < top_g:
           extra = rng.choice(n, size=top_g, replace=False)
           idxs = np.unique(np.concatenate((idxs, extra)))[:top_g]
        improved=False
        # maybe track pairs tried in a set to avoid immediate repeat; use dictionary? Could skip.
        for a in range(idxs.size):
           if time.time() > deadline: break

              if time.time() > deadline: break
              j=int(idxs[b]); hj=h[j]
              low = max(-hi, -(1.0-hj)); high = min(1.0-hi, hj)
              if high-low <1e-13: continue

              # quick: if c at low/high and 0? best_t optim

                  # clip small negatives

              failures=0

```

```
def periodic_sa(p,K,seed,max_iters=200000):
 m=p//2; full=1<<p
 rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
 # precompute valid masks? list valid_masks[r]=(1<<(p-r))-1
 best=K+1
 for restart in range(20):
   # random mask weight m
   idxs=rng.choice(p,m,replace=False)
   x=0; for i in idxs: x|=1<<i
   ones=[int(i) for i in idxs]; zeros=[i for i in range(p) if i not in ones]
   cur=eval_cap(x,p,K+1)
   if cur<=K: return x
   T=0.02
   no_improve=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
     # swap
     a=int(rng.integers(len(ones))); b=int(rng.integers(len(zeros)))
     i=ones[a]; j=zeros[b]
     newx=x^(1<<i)^(1<<j)
     newcur=eval_cap(newx,p,K+1)
     if newcur<cur or (T>0 and rng.random()<math.exp(-(newcur-cur)/T)):
        x=newx; cur=newcur
        # update lists: remove i from ones add zeros; remove j from zeros add ones
        if newcur<cur: no_improve=0
        else: no_improve+=1
        if cur<=K: return x
        if newcur<cur: ...
     else: no_improve+=1
     if no_improve>2000:
        # perturb: swap 2 random pairs even if worse
        for _ in range(3): choose swap, accept regardless and update
        T *=0.9
        no_improve=0
   if T<0.001: break
 return None
```

Pair search objective: c vector from FFT. In update c = c + t_best*Ld, Ld = L[i]-L[j] (raw derivative of c vector wrt h_i/h_j). Cross terms: We add +t^2 for lags where i,j pair in same term. For d=j-i positive: raw c_d contains h_i(1-h_j). Change quadratic +t^2. For d=i-j negative: contains h_j(1-h_i). Change? Let i<j. If h_i +=t,h_j-=t. Original j(1-i); new (j-t)(1-(i+t)) = j - ji - t(j - i) -? expand: (j-t)(1-i-t)=j - ji - jt - t + it + t^2. Linear -t(j-i), quadratic +t^2. Derivative L_i for negative d includes -h_j (if i second), L_j includes 1-h_i (if j first); L_i-L_j = -h_j - (1-h_i) = h_i - h_j -1. Cross derivative from true: derivative wrt i? term wrt i = -h_j; wrt j=1-h_i; move derivative = -h_j*t - (1-h_i)*(-t)? Wait j change -t, derivative wrt j=1-h_i, contribution -t*(1-h_i), total t(h_i - h_j -1) matches. quadratic +t^2. Good.
Self d=0: two terms. Quadratic -2t^2. Good.

full=(1<<p)-1
best=0
for r in range(1,p):
  valid=(1<<(p-r))-1
  sh=(xmask<<r)&full
  d=(xmask & ~sh & valid).bit_count()
  if d>best: best=d
  if cap is not None and best>cap: return best
  e=((full ^ xmask) & sh & valid).bit_count()
  if e>best: best=e
  if cap is not None and best>cap: return best
return best
```

def try_periodic_milp(p,K,time_limit=8):
    try:
       from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint
    except Exception:
       return None
    m=p//2
    npairs=p*(p-1)//2
    nv=p+npairs
    cobj=np.zeros(nv)
    lb=np.zeros(nv); ub=np.ones(nv)
    integrality=np.empty(nv,int); integrality[:p]=1; integrality[p:]=0
    # A_eq
    Aeq=np.zeros((1,nv)); Aeq[0,:p]=1.0
    beq=np.array([m])
    rows=[]; lrow=[]; urow=[]
    z_start=p
    # helper add z index
    z_idx=0
    for r in range(1,p):
       cnt=p-r
       Zd=z_start+z_idx
       # D
       row=np.zeros(nv)
       for i in range(cnt):
          row[i]=1.0
          row[Zd+i]=-1.0
       rows append row; lrow -inf; urow K
       z_idx += cnt
       # E

def focused_optimize(h,n,deadline, do_lbfgs=True):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); c=compute_c5_direct(h,n)
    t_start=time.time()
    # Pair phase 1
    t_pair1 = min(240, (deadline - time.time())*0.35)
    if t_pair1 > 30:
       h,c = pair_local_search(h,n,time.time()+t_pair1)
    # L-BFGS phase
    if do_lbfgs and time.time() < deadline - 120 and n <= 1200:
       t_lb = min(180, (deadline - time.time())*0.35)
       if t_lb > 60:
          h2,c2 = run_active_lbfgs(h,n,time.time()+t_lb)
          if c2 < c: h,c=h2,c2
    # Pair phase 2 remaining
    if time.time() < deadline - 20:
       h,c = pair_local_search(h,n,deadline-3)
    return h,c
```

def refine_candidate(h,n,deadline):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c5_direct(h,n)
    # pair 1 with time 40% remaining
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    t1=min(300, rem*0.45)
    if t1>30:
       h,c=pair_local_search(h,n,time.time()+t1)
    # L-BFGS if n<=1200 and time left >100
    if time.time() < deadline-100 and n <= 1200:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       t2=min(180, rem*0.35)
       if t2>60:
          h2,c2=run_active_lbfgs(h,n,time.time()+t2)
          if c2 < c - 1e-15:
             h,c=h2,c2
    # pair 2 rest
    if time.time() < deadline-10:
       h,c=pair_local_search(h,n,deadline-2)
    return h,c
```

Let's implement pair search more concretely:
```
def pair_local_search(h,n,deadline, top_g=40, stall_s=30):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c5_fft(h,n)
    best=float(c.max())
    rng=np.random.default_rng(987654321)
    no_improve_start=time.time()
    imp_count=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        L=build_L(h,n)
        active=select_lags(c,n, max_active=48)
        grad = L[:, active].mean(axis=1) * (2.0/n)
        top = np.argsort(-np.abs(grad))[:top_g]
        # add random 10
        if top.size < top_g:
            extra = rng.choice(n, size=top_g-top.size, replace=False)
            top = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, extra)))[:top_g]
        else:
            extra = rng.choice(n, size=10, replace=False)
            top = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, extra)))[:top_g]
        improved=False
        # order pairs perhaps random shuffle to diversify
        pairs=[]?
        for a in range(top.size):
          i=int(top[a]); hi=h[i]
          for b in range(a+1, top.size):
             if time.time()>deadline: break
             j=int(top[b]); hj=h[j]
             low=max(-hi, -(1.0-hj)); high=min(1.0-hi, hj)
             if high-low <1e-13: continue
             Ld=L[i]-L[j]
             tbest,vbest=optimize_pair_t(c,Ld,n,i,j,low,high)
             if vbest < best - 1e-12:
                 # apply
                 t=tbest
                 # rescale t to safe
                 if t < low: t=low
                 if t > high: t=high
                 new_hi=h[i]+t
                 if new_hi<0: t= -h[i]; new_hi=0.0
                 elif new_hi>1: t=1.0-h[i]; new_hi=1.0
                 new_hj=h[j]-t
                 if new_hj<0 or new_hj>1:
                    # recompute t bounds? skip
                    pass
                 h[i]=new_hi
                 h[j]=new_hj
                 # Update c exact using t (not t_actual? use new_hi - old h[i])
                 t_update=new_hi - (h[i] if? old) Need store old_i.
```

`optimize_pair_t` implementation:
```
def optimize_pair_t(c0,Ld,n,i,j,low,high):
    idx0=n-1; idxp=(j-i)+idx0; idxn=(i-j)+idx0
    best_t=0.0; best_val=float(c0.max())
    temp=np.empty_like(c0)
    span=high-low
    if span <= 1e-14: return 0,best_val
    # Coarse points: use linspace and 0
    ts=np.linspace(low, high, 33)
    # include zero
    zero = 0.0
    # we can not use np.concatenate to avoid closure? okay
    for t in ts:
       if abs(t) < 1e-15: continue
       val=eval_pair_point(temp,c0,Ld,t,idx0,idxp,idxn)
       if val < best_val - 1e-15:
          best_val=val; best_t=t
    # refine
    scale=span/16.0
    for _ in range(3):
       lo=max(low,best_t-scale); hi=min(high,best_t+scale)
       if hi-lo <= 1e-13: break
       ts2=np.linspace(lo,hi,17)
       local_best_t=best_t; local_best_val=best_val
       for t in ts2: ...
       if local_best_t == best_t and local_best_val == best_val: break
       best_t=local_best_t; best_val=local_best_val
       scale *= 0.4
    # ensure t=0 considered
    if best_val > c0.max(): best=0
    return best_t,best_val
```

def apply_pair_move(h,c,Ld,n,i,j,t):
   old_i=h[i]; old_j=h[j]
   h[i]=old_i+t; h[j]=old_j-t
   if h[i]<0: h[i]=0.0; t=h[i]-old_i; h[j]=old_j-t
   elif h[i]>1: h[i]=1.0; t=h[i]-old_i; h[j]=old_j-t
   if h[j]<0: h[j]=0.0; t=old_j-h[j]; h[i]=old_i+t
   elif h[j]>1: h[j]=1.0; t=clamp; h[i]=old_i+t
   # use actual t_update = h[i]-old_i for c

   c = c + tu*Ld

   idxp=(j-i)+n-1; c[idxp]+=v

   # clip small negative
   mask=(c<0)&(c>-1e-11); c[mask]=0
   return h,c

`eval_pair_point` uses Ls:
```
def eval_pair_point(temp,c0,Ls,t,dx,idx0,idxp,idxn):
    temp[:] = c0
    temp += t * Ls
    v = t*t*dx
    temp[idx0] -= 2.0*v
    temp[idxp] += v
    if idxn != idxp:
        temp[idxn] += v
    return float(temp.max())
```

def perturb_h(h,n,scale,rng):
    x=h.copy()
    k=max(4, int(0.05*n))
    idx=rng.choice(n,k,replace=False)
    noise=(rng.random(k)-0.5)*2.0*scale
    x[idx]+=noise
    return project_feasible(x,n)
```

    for i in range(len(cands)):
      best=i
      for j in range(i+1,len(cands)):

Implement periodic_random_restart robust:
```
def periodic_random_restart(p,K,seed,max_iters=5000):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    m=p//2
    for r in range(6):
       idxs=list(rng.choice(p,m,replace=False))
       ones=list(idxs)
       x=0
       for i in ones: x <<=1? no bit.
       full=...
       zeros=[i for i in range(p) if i not in ones] # set
       cur=periodic_max_de(x,p,K+1)
       if cur<=K: return x
       no_improve=0
       for it in range(max_iters):
         a=int(rng.integers(len(ones))); b=int(rng.integers(len(zeros)))
         i=ones[a]; j=zeros[b]
         newx = x ^ (1<<i) ^ (1<<j)
         new = periodic_max_de(newx,p,K+1)
         accept=False
         if new < cur:
            accept=True; no_improve=0
         elif new == cur:
            accept=True; no_improve=min(no_improve+1,1000)
         elif cur == K and new <=K+1? no
         elif cur < K+1 and new == cur+1 and rng.random()<0.05:
            accept=True; no_improve+=1
         if accept:
            x=newx; cur=new
            # update lists: i one->zero, j zero->one
            # remove from lists
            ones[a]=j; zeros[b]=i  # O(1) because indices known
            if cur<=K: return x
         if no_improve > 500: break
    return None
```
Replacing positions works because we know a,b. Good.

`ones` and `zeros` lists remain correct after swap: swap values at positions a,b. Since i removes from ones, j from zeros; we put j in ones[a], i in zeros[b]. Good.

`rng.choice(p,m,replace=False)` returns numpy array; convert list for ints. `i not in ones` O(p) for zeros list; p≤48 okay.

def refine_candidate(h,n,deadline):
    if deadline - time.time() < 30:
       h=project_feasible(h,n); c=compute_c5_direct(h,n); return h,c
    h=project_feasible(h,n); c=compute_c5_direct(h,n)
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    t1=min(360, rem*0.45)
    if t1 > 30:
       h,c=pair_local_search(h,n,time.time()+t1)
    if time.time() < deadline - 120 and n <= 1152:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       t2=min(180, rem*0.35)
       if t2 > 60:
          h2,c2=run_active_lbfgs(h,n,time.time()+t2)

    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
       h,c=pair_local_search(h,n,deadline-2)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c5_direct(h,n)
    return h,c
```

```

while np.clip(y+hi,0,1).sum() < target: hi += max(1.0, target)
```

    Lmat = np.empty((n,2*n-1), dtype=float)
    om = 1.0 - h

    mrev = -rev
    L1 = 2*n-1

        row.fill(0.0)
        row[n-1-i : n-1+i+1] = om  # end 2n-1-i = n-1+i+1
        row[i : i+n] += mrev
    return Lmat

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def compute_c5_vec_direct(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx


def compute_c5_direct(h, n):
    c = compute_c5_vec_direct(h, n)
    return float(np.max(c))


def compute_c5_fft(h, n):
    M = 1
    while M < 4 * n:
        M <<= 1
    a = np.zeros(M, dtype=float)
    b = np.zeros(M, dtype=float)
    a[:n] = h
    b[:n] = 1.0 - h
    A = np.fft.rfft(a)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(A * np.conj(B), n=M)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    out = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    out[0:n - 1] = corr[M - (n - 1):M]
    out[n - 1] = corr[0]
    out[n:] = corr[1:n]
    out *= 2.0 / float(n)
    bad = (out < 0.0) & (out > -1e-10)
    if np.any(bad):
        out[bad] = 0.0
    return out


def project_feasible(h, n):
    x = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if x.shape[0] != n:
        x = np.resize(x, n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    if np.all(x >= -1e-12) and np.all(x <= 1.0 + 1e-12) and abs(float(x.sum()) - target) < 1e-11:
        return np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)

    y = x.copy()
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(8):
        if np.clip(y + lo, 0.0, 1.0).sum() <= target:
            break
        lo -= max(1.0, target)
    for _ in range(8):
        if np.clip(y + hi, 0.0, 1.0).sum() >= target:
            break
        hi += max(1.0, target)

    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(y + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    x = np.clip(y + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(30):
        diff = target - float(x.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - x
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            x += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = float(x.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            x += (diff / tot) * x
    return np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)


def upsample_h(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    if n_old == n_new:
        return np.array(h_old, dtype=float).copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    return np.interp(new, old, np.asarray(h_old, dtype=float))


def select_lags(c, n, tol_rel=1e-5, tol_abs=1e-9, max_active=72, top_q=16):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    tol = max(tol_abs, tol_rel * m)
    cand = np.where(c >= m - tol)[0]
    if cand.size == 0:
        cand = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    if cand.size > max_active:
        order = np.argsort(-c[cand])[:max_active]
        cand = cand[order]
    else:
        if cand.size < top_q:
            top = np.argsort(-c)[:top_q]
            cand = np.unique(np.concatenate((cand, top)))
        if cand.size > max_active:
            order = np.argsort(-c[cand])[:max_active]
            cand = cand[order]
    return cand


def sparse_grad_weighted(h, n, lags, weights):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for w, idx in zip(weights, lags):
        if w <= 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(idx) - (n - 1)
        wm = float(w) * dx
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            g[0:nv] += wm * (1.0 - h[k:n])
            g[k:n] += wm * (-h[0:nv])
        else:
            kk = -k
            nv = n - kk
            g[kk:n] += wm * (1.0 - h[0:nv])
            g[0:nv] += wm * (-h[kk:n])
    return g


def active_smooth_obj(h, n, lags, alpha, mu):
    c = compute_c5_fft(h, n)
    vals = c[lags]
    if vals.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = np.max(vals)
    s = alpha * (vals - m)
    logsum = np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s)))
    val = float(m + logsum / alpha)
    if mu != 0.0:
        d = float(h.sum()) - float(n) / 2.0
        val += mu * d * d
    return val


def active_smooth_grad(h, n, lags, alpha, mu):
    c = compute_c5_fft(h, n)
    vals = c[lags]
    if vals.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n)
    m = np.max(vals)
    s = alpha * (vals - m)
    w = np.exp(s)
    wsum = float(w.sum())
    if wsum <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(vals.size, dtype=float) / vals.size
    else:
        w = w / wsum
    g = sparse_grad_weighted(h, n, lags, w)
    if mu != 0.0:
        d = float(h.sum()) - float(n) / 2.0
        g += (2.0 * mu * d)
    return g


def run_active_lbfgs(h, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best = compute_c5_direct(h, n)
    if best <= 0.38080:
        return h, best
    configs = [
        (50.0, 0.05, 16),
        (200.0, 0.5, 24),
        (1000.0, 5.0, 32),
        (5000.0, 50.0, 40),
        (20000.0, 500.0, 48),
        (100000.0, 5000.0, 48),
    ]
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    for alpha, mu, maxa in configs:
        if time.time() > deadline - 4:
            break
        c = compute_c5_fft(h, n)
        lags = select_lags(c, n, max_active=maxa)
        res = None
        try:
            res = minimize(
                active_smooth_obj,
                h,
                jac=active_smooth_grad,
                args=(n, lags, alpha, mu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": 700,
                    "maxfun": 1400,
                    "ftol": 1e-18,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxcor": 30,
                    "maxls": 50,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
            hnew = project_feasible(res.x, n)
            cnew = compute_c5_direct(hnew, n)
            if cnew < best - 1e-15:
                best = cnew
                h = hnew
    return h, best


def build_L(h, n):
    Lmat = np.empty((n, 2 * n - 1), dtype=float)
    om = 1.0 - h
    rev = h[::-1]
    mrev = -rev
    for i in range(n):
        row = Lmat[i]
        row.fill(0.0)
        row[n - 1 - i:2 * n - 1 - i] = om
        row[i:i + n] += mrev
    return Lmat


def eval_pair_point(temp, c0, Ls, t, dx, idx0, idxp, idxn):
    temp[:] = c0
    temp += t * Ls
    v = t * t * dx
    temp[idx0] -= 2.0 * v
    temp[idxp] += v
    if idxn != idxp:
        temp[idxn] += v
    return float(np.max(temp))


def optimize_pair_t(c0, Ld, n, i, j, low, high):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    idx0 = n - 1
    idxp = (j - i) + idx0
    idxn = (i - j) + idx0
    best_val = float(np.max(c0))
    best_t = 0.0
    span = high - low
    if span <= 1e-13:
        return best_t, best_val
    Ls = Ld * dx
    temp = np.empty_like(c0)
    ts = np.linspace(low, high, 33)
    for t in ts:
        if abs(t) <= 1e-15:
            continue
        val = eval_pair_point(temp, c0, Ls, float(t), dx, idx0, idxp, idxn)
        if val < best_val - 1e-15:
            best_val = val
            best_t = float(t)
    scale = span / 16.0
    for _ in range(3):
        lo = max(low, best_t - scale)
        hi = min(high, best_t + scale)
        if hi - lo <= 1e-13:
            break
        ts2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, 17)
        local_best_t = best_t
        local_best_val = best_val
        for t in ts2:
            if abs(t) <= 1e-15:
                continue
            val = eval_pair_point(temp, c0, Ls, float(t), dx, idx0, idxp, idxn)
            if val < local_best_val - 1e-15:
                local_best_val = val
                local_best_t = float(t)
        if local_best_t == best_t and local_best_val == best_val:
            break
        best_t = local_best_t
        best_val = local_best_val
        scale *= 0.4
    return best_t, best_val


def apply_pair_update(h, c, Ld, n, i, j, t):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    old_i = h[i]
    old_j = h[j]
    h[i] = old_i + t
    h[j] = old_j - t
    if h[i] < 0.0 or h[i] > 1.0 or h[j] < 0.0 or h[j] > 1.0:
        # very tiny numerical cleanup, then enforce exact sum on this pair
        if h[i] < 0.0:
            h[i] = 0.0
        elif h[i] > 1.0:
            h[i] = 1.0
        if h[j] < 0.0:
            h[j] = 0.0
        elif h[j] > 1.0:
            h[j] = 1.0
    t_actual = h[i] - old_i
    h[j] = old_j - t_actual
    c = c + t_actual * dx * Ld
    v = t_actual * t_actual * dx
    idx0 = n - 1
    idxp = (j - i) + idx0
    idxn = (i - j) + idx0
    c[idx0] -= 2.0 * v
    c[idxp] += v
    if idxn != idxp:
        c[idxn] += v
    bad = (c < 0.0) & (c > -1e-11)
    if np.any(bad):
        c[bad] = 0.0
    return h, c


def pair_local_search(h, n, deadline, top_g=44, stall_s=25):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c5_fft(h, n)
    best = float(np.max(c))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)
    fails = 0
    imp_count = 0
    no_improve_start = time.time()

    while time.time() < deadline:
        L = build_L(h, n)
        active = select_lags(c, n, max_active=56)
        grad = L[:, active].mean(axis=1) * (2.0 / float(n))
        top = np.argsort(-np.abs(grad))[:top_g]
        extra_count = 12 + 18 * fails
        if extra_count > 0 and n > top.size:
            extra = rng.choice(n, size=extra_count, replace=False)
            top = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, extra)))[:top_g]
        if top.size == 0:
            break

        improved = False
        for a in range(top.size):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            i = int(top[a])
            hi = h[i]
            for b in range(a + 1, top.size):
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                j = int(top[b])
                hj = h[j]
                low = max(-hi, hj - 1.0)
                high = min(1.0 - hi, hj)
                if high - low < 1e-13:
                    continue
                Ld = L[i] - L[j]
                tbest, vbest = optimize_pair_t(c, Ld, n, i, j, low, high)
                if vbest < best - 1e-12:
                    eps = 1e-13 * max(1.0, high - low)
                    t = min(max(tbest, low + eps), high - eps)
                    h, c = apply_pair_update(h, c, Ld, n, i, j, float(t))
                    best = float(np.max(c))
                    imp_count += 1
                    improved = True
                    no_improve_start = time.time()
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if improved:
            fails = 0
            if imp_count % 12 == 0:
                c = compute_c5_fft(h, n)
                best = float(np.max(c))
            continue

        # If the focused set is locally tight, try some random pairs before giving up.
        random_improved = False
        for _ in range(500):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            j = int(rng.integers(n))
            if i == j:
                continue
            hi = h[i]
            hj = h[j]
            low = max(-hi, hj - 1.0)
            high = min(1.0 - hi, hj)
            if high - low < 1e-13:
                continue
            Ld = L[i] - L[j]
            tbest, vbest = optimize_pair_t(c, Ld, n, i, j, low, high)
            if vbest < best - 1e-12:
                eps = 1e-13 * max(1.0, high - low)
                t = min(max(tbest, low + eps), high - eps)
                h, c = apply_pair_update(h, c, Ld, n, i, j, float(t))
                best = float(np.max(c))
                imp_count += 1
                fails = 0
                no_improve_start = time.time()
                random_improved = True
                break

        if random_improved:
            if imp_count % 12 == 0:
                c = compute_c5_fft(h, n)
                best = float(np.max(c))
            continue

        fails += 1
        if fails >= 3 or time.time() - no_improve_start > stall_s:
            break

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    cvec = compute_c5_vec_direct(h, n)
    best = float(np.max(cvec))
    return h, best


def order_candidates(cands):
    for i in range(len(cands)):
        best_i = i
        for j in range(i + 1, len(cands)):
            if cands[j][2] < cands[best_i][2]:
                best_i = j
        if best_i != i:
            tmp = cands[i]
            cands[i] = cands[best_i]
            cands[best_i] = tmp
    return cands


def refine_candidate(h, n, deadline):
    if deadline - time.time() <= 30.0:
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = compute_c5_direct(h, n)
        return h, c

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c5_direct(h, n)

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    t1 = min(360.0, rem * 0.45)
    if t1 > 30.0:
        h, c = pair_local_search(h, n, time.time() + t1)

    if time.time() < deadline - 120.0 and n <= 1152:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        t2 = min(180.0, rem * 0.35)
        if t2 > 60.0:
            h2, c2 = run_active_lbfgs(h, n, time.time() + t2)
            if c2 < c - 1e-15:
                h, c = h2, c2

    if time.time() < deadline - 15.0:
        h, c = pair_local_search(h, n, deadline - 2.0)

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c5_direct(h, n)
    return h, c


def perturb_h(h, n, scale, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    k = max(8, int(0.04 * n) + 1)
    idx = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
    noise = (rng.random(k) - 0.5) * (2.0 * scale)
    x[idx] += noise
    return project_feasible(x, n)


def periodic_max_de(xmask, p, cap=None):
    full = (1 << p) - 1
    best = 0
    for r in range(1, p):
        valid = (1 << (p - r)) - 1
        sh = (xmask << r) & full
        d = (xmask & (~sh) & valid).bit_count()
        if d > best:
            best = d
        if cap is not None and best > cap:
            return best
        e = ((full ^ xmask) & sh & valid).bit_count()
        if e > best:
            best = e
        if cap is not None and best > cap:
            return best
    return best


def periodic_random_restart(p, K, seed, max_iters=6000):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    m = p // 2
    for _ in range(6):
        idxs = list(rng.choice(p, m, replace=False))
        ones = list(idxs)
        x = 0
        for i in ones:
            x |= (1 << i)
        sones = set(ones)
        zeros = [i for i in range(p) if i not in sones]
        cur = periodic_max_de(x, p, K + 1)
        if cur <= K:
            return x
        no_improve = 0
        for _ in range(max_iters):
            if not ones or not zeros:
                break
            a = int(rng.integers(len(ones)))
            b = int(rng.integers(len(zeros)))
            i = ones[a]
            j = zeros[b]
            newx = x ^ (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
            newcur = periodic_max_de(newx, p, K + 1)
            accept = False
            if newcur < cur:
                accept = True
                no_improve = 0
            elif newcur == cur:
                accept = True
                no_improve += 1
            elif cur < K + 1 and newcur == cur + 1 and rng.random() < 0.04:
                accept = True
                no_improve += 1
            if accept:
                x = newx
                cur = newcur
                ones[a] = j
                zeros[b] = i
                if cur <= K:
                    return x
            if no_improve > 700:
                break
    return None


def try_periodic_milp(p, K, time_limit=6.0):
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    except Exception:
        return None
    m = p // 2
    npairs = p * (p - 1) // 2
    nv = p + npairs
    cobj = np.zeros(nv, dtype=float)
    lb = np.zeros(nv, dtype=float)
    ub = np.ones(nv, dtype=float)
    integrality = np.empty(nv, dtype=int)
    integrality[:p] = 1
    integrality[p:] = 0

    rows = []
    lbs = []
    ubs = []

    row = np.zeros(nv, dtype=float)
    row[:p] = 1.0
    rows.append(row)
    lbs.append(float(m))
    ubs.append(float(m))

    z = p
    for r in range(1, p):
        Drow = np.zeros(nv, dtype=float)
        Erow = np.zeros(nv, dtype=float)
        cnt = p - r
        for _i in range(cnt):
            zi = z
            z += 1
            # z <= b_i
            row = np.zeros(nv, dtype=float)
            row[zi] = 1.0
            row[_i] = -1.0
            rows.append(row)
            lbs.append(-np.inf)
            ubs.append(0.0)
            # z <= b_{i+r}
            row = np.zeros(nv, dtype=float)
            row[zi] = 1.0
            row[_i + r] = -1.0
            rows.append(row)
            lbs.append(-np.inf)
            ubs.append(0.0)
            # z >= b_i + b_{i+r} - 1  => z - b_i - b_{i+r} <= -1
            row = np.zeros(nv, dtype=float)
            row[zi] = 1.0
            row[_i] = -1.0
            row[_i + r] = -1.0
            rows.append(row)
            lbs.append(-np.inf)
            ubs.append(-1.0)

            Drow[_i] += 1.0
            Drow[zi] -= 1.0
            Erow[_i + r] += 1.0
            Erow[zi] -= 1.0
        rows.append(Drow)
        lbs.append(-np.inf)
        ubs.append(float(K))
        rows.append(Erow)
        lbs.append(-np.inf)
        ubs.append(float(K))

    A = np.vstack(rows) if rows else np.empty((0, nv), dtype=float)
    cons = LinearConstraint(A, np.asarray(lbs, dtype=float), np.asarray(ubs, dtype=float))
    try:
        bnds = Bounds(lb, ub)
    except Exception:
        bnds = list(zip(lb, ub))

    res = None
    try:
        res = milp(
            cobj,
            constraints=cons,
            integrality=integrality,
            bounds=bnds,
            options={"time_limit": max(1, int(time_limit))},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or not hasattr(res, "x") or res.x is None:
        return None
    b = np.round(res.x[:p]).astype(float)
    if abs(float(b.sum()) - float(m)) > 1e-6:
        return None
    x = 0
    for i in range(p):
        if b[i] > 0.5:
            x |= (1 << i)
    md = periodic_max_de(x, p)
    if md <= K:
        return x
    return None


def periodic_to_candidate(mask, p, target_n=1152):
    b = np.zeros(p, dtype=float)
    for i in range(p):
        b[i] = float((mask >> i) & 1)
    if abs(float(b.sum()) - float(p // 2)) > 1e-12:
        return None
    mult = max(1, target_n // p)
    n = p * mult
    h = np.repeat(b, mult)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c5_direct(h, n)
    return h, n, c


def try_periodic_search(deadline, seed):
    best = None
    best_c = 1.0
    # Keep MILP instances small enough for the one-CPU budget.
    p_list = [48, 40, 36, 32, 28, 24, 20, 16]
    for p in p_list:
        if time.time() > deadline - 8.0:
            break
        Ktarget = int((0.3808 - 1e-12) * p / 2.0)
        if Ktarget < 0:
            continue

        if time.time() < deadline - 20.0:
            mask = periodic_random_restart(p, Ktarget, seed + 101 * p, max_iters=3500)
            if mask is not None:
                md = periodic_max_de(mask, p)
                cc = 2.0 * float(md) / float(p)
                if cc < best_c:
                    best_c = cc
                    best = (mask, p)

        if time.time() < deadline - 10.0:
            tl = min(6.0, deadline - time.time() - 6.0)
            if tl > 1.5:
                mask = try_periodic_milp(p, Ktarget, tl)
                if mask is not None:
                    md = periodic_max_de(mask, p)
                    cc = 2.0 * float(md) / float(p)
                    if cc < best_c:
                        best_c = cc
                        best = (mask, p)

        if best is not None and best_c <= 0.3750:
            break
    return best


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
        have_parent = True
    except Exception:
        h0 = np.full(512, 0.5, dtype=float)
        n0 = 512
        have_parent = False

    if n0 < 2:
        h0 = np.full(256, 0.5, dtype=float)
        n0 = 256
    if n0 % 2 == 1:
        h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
        h0 = upsample_h(h0, n0, n0 + 1)
        n0 += 1

    cand_list = []

    # A good working resolution near the parent size.
    parent_work = int(n0)
    if parent_work < 128 or parent_work > 1152 or parent_work % 2 == 1:
        parent_work = 1000
    hp = upsample_h(h0, n0, parent_work)
    hp = project_feasible(hp, parent_work)
    cp = compute_c5_direct(hp, parent_work)
    cand_list.append((hp, parent_work, cp))

    # A finer candidate, useful for escaping the coarse-grid local optimum.
    fine_n = 1000
    if fine_n == parent_work:
        fine_n = 1152
    if fine_n <= 1152 and fine_n >= 64:
        hf = upsample_h(h0, n0, fine_n)
        hf = project_feasible(hf, fine_n)
        cf = compute_c5_direct(hf, fine_n)
        cand_list.append((hf, fine_n, cf))

    # Periodic-construction search: if it succeeds, it can be a strong independent start.
    periodic_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + min(90.0, max(30.0, 0.09 * float(budget_s))))
    periodic = try_periodic_search(periodic_deadline, seed)
    if periodic is not None:
        per = periodic_to_candidate(periodic[0], periodic[1], target_n=1152)
        if per is not None:
            cand_list.append(per)

    if not cand_list:
        hu = project_feasible(np.full(512, 0.5, dtype=float), 512)
        cu = compute_c5_direct(hu, 512)
        cand_list.append((hu, 512, cu))

    cand_list = order_candidates(cand_list)

    best_h = np.array(cand_list[0][0], dtype=float).copy()
    best_n = int(cand_list[0][1])
    best_c = float(cand_list[0][2])

    # Refine best initial candidate, then the next best ones if time remains.
    sub_deadlines = []
    if len(cand_list) >= 3:
        sub_deadlines = [deadline - 300.0, min(deadline - 60.0, time.time() + 300.0), min(deadline - 20.0, time.time() + 120.0)]
    elif len(cand_list) == 2:
        sub_deadlines = [deadline - 150.0, min(deadline - 20.0, time.time() + 220.0)]
    else:
        sub_deadlines = [deadline - 10.0]

    for idx in range(len(cand_list)):
        if time.time() > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        sub_dead = max(time.time() + 50.0, min(sub_deadlines[idx], deadline - 10.0))
        if sub_dead < time.time() + 35.0:
            continue
        h_r, n_r, c_r = refine_candidate(cand_list[idx][0], cand_list[idx][1], sub_dead)
        if c_r < best_c:
            best_h = np.array(h_r, dtype=float).copy()
            best_n = int(n_r)
            best_c = float(c_r)

    # Small perturbation epochs to escape stubborn two-point local minima.
    scales = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10)
    pseed = int(seed) + 7777
    for scale in scales:
        if time.time() > deadline - 130.0:
            break
        hp2 = perturb_h(best_h, best_n, scale, pseed)
        pseed += 13
        cp2 = compute_c5_direct(hp2, best_n)
        if cp2 < best_c + 0.012:
            sub_dead = min(deadline - 10.0, time.time() + 90.0)
            if sub_dead - time.time() > 35.0:
                h_r, c_r = refine_candidate(hp2, best_n, sub_dead)
                if c_r < best_c:
                    best_h = np.array(h_r, dtype=float).copy()
                    best_c = float(c_r)

    # Final validation and exact feasible projection.
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5_direct(best_h, best_n)
    if best_h.size != best_n:
        best_h = np.resize(best_h, best_n)
        best_c = compute_c5_direct(best_h, best_n)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
